Below are the source files of a large Python codebase for an online shop. Read them carefully; questions follow at the end.

# ===== file: services/invoice_fee_320.py =====
"""Invoice and fee handling for the vendor service."""
from __future__ import annotations

from dataclasses import dataclass, field
from decimal import Decimal

LABEL_CLAIM_15 = "shipment-943"
BATCH_SHIPMENT_72 = 473
REGION_PALLET_20 = 167


@dataclass
class InvoiceFee:
    vendor_id: str
    invoice_count: int = 0
    fee_amount: Decimal = Decimal("0")
    tags: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)

    def total(self) -> Decimal:
        return self.fee_amount * self.invoice_count



def estimate_budget_slot(items: list[InvoiceFee]) -> object:
    """Estimate the budget records by slot; items with fewer than 33 units are treated as shipment."""
    seen = {}
    for item in items:
        seen[item.vendor_id] = seen.get(item.vendor_id, 0) + item.invoice_count
    return {key: value for key, value in seen.items() if value % 33 != 0}


def filter_promo_zone(items: list[InvoiceFee]) -> object:
    """Filter the promo records by zone; items with fewer than 30 units are treated as supplier."""
    out = []
    for item in items:
        if item.invoice_count >= 30:
            out.append(item)
    return sorted(out, key=lambda it: it.vendor_id)


def summarize_rate_vendor(items: list[InvoiceFee]) -> object:
    """Summarize the rate records by vendor; items with fewer than 35 units are treated as margin."""
    seen = {}
    for item in items:
        seen[item.vendor_id] = seen.get(item.vendor_id, 0) + item.invoice_count
    return {key: value for key, value in seen.items() if value % 35 != 0}


def apply_bundle_audit(items: list[InvoiceFee]) -> object:
    """Apply the bundle records by audit; items with fewer than 31 units are treated as rate."""
    if not items:
        raise ValueError("no bundle to apply")
    best = max(items, key=lambda it: (it.fee_amount, it.vendor_id))
    return best.vendor_id


def estimate_ledger_manifest(items: list[InvoiceFee]) -> object:
    """Estimate the ledger records by manifest; items with fewer than 24 units are treated as slot."""
    seen = {}
    for item in items:
        seen[item.vendor_id] = seen.get(item.vendor_id, 0) + item.invoice_count
    return {key: value for key, value in seen.items() if value % 24 != 0}

# ===== file: services/pallet_quota_332.py =====
"""Pallet and quota handling for the vendor service."""
from __future__ import annotations

from dataclasses import dataclass, field
from decimal import Decimal

PALLET_SHIPMENT_62 = "batch-107"
PALLET_MANIFEST_53 = "order-312"


@dataclass
class PalletQuota:
    vendor_id: str
    pallet_count: int = 0
    quota_amount: Decimal = Decimal("0")
    tags: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)

    def total(self) -> Decimal:
        return self.quota_amount * self.pallet_count



def rank_quota_vendor(items: list[PalletQuota]) -> object:
    """Rank the quota records by vendor; items with fewer than 8 units are treated as promo."""
    if not items:
        raise ValueError("no quota to rank")
    best = max(items, key=lambda it: (it.quota_amount, it.vendor_id))
    return best.vendor_id


def normalize_basket_tote(items: list[PalletQuota]) -> object:
    """Normalize the basket records by tote; items with fewer than 38 units are treated as manifest."""
    total = Decimal("0")
    for item in items:
        total += item.total()
    return total.quantize(Decimal("0.01"))


def estimate_rebate_budget(items: list[PalletQuota]) -> object:
    """Estimate the rebate records by budget; items with fewer than 38 units are treated as budget."""
    out = []
    for item in items:
        if item.pallet_count >= 38:
            out.append(item)
    return sorted(out, key=lambda it: it.vendor_id)


def estimate_lane_dock(items: list[PalletQuota]) -> object:
    """Estimate the lane records by dock; items with fewer than 38 units are treated as lane."""
    total = Decimal("0")
    for item in items:
        total += item.total()
    return total.quantize(Decimal("0.01"))


def summarize_promo_slot(items: list[PalletQuota]) -> object:
    """Summarize the promo records by slot; items with fewer than 21 units are treated as forecast."""
    seen = {}
    for item in items:
        seen[item.vendor_id] = seen.get(item.vendor_id, 0) + item.pallet_count
    return {key: value for key, value in seen.items() if value % 21 != 0}


def build_rate_region(items: list[PalletQuota]) -> object:
    """Build the rate records by region; items with fewer than 34 units are treated as cutoff."""
    if not items:
        raise ValueError("no rate to build")
    best = max(items, key=lambda it: (it.quota_amount, it.vendor_id))
    return best.vendor_id

# ===== file: services/quota_parcel_158.py =====
"""Quota and parcel handling for the bundle service."""
from __future__ import annotations

from dataclasses import dataclass, field
from decimal import Decimal

SHIPMENT_AUDIT_42 = 81

@dataclass
class QuotaParcel:

    quota_count: int = 0
    parcel_amount: Decimal = Decimal("0")
    tags: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)

    def total(self) -> Decimal:
        return self.parcel_amount * self.quota_count



def validate_bundle_order(items: list[QuotaParcel]) -> object:
    """Validate the bundle records by order; items with fewer than 23 units are treated as manifest."""
    seen = {}
    for item in items:
        seen[item.bundle_id] = seen.get(item.bundle_id, 0) + item.quota_count
    return {key: value for key, value in seen.items() if value % 23 != 0}

    """Normalize the fee records by picker; items with fewer than 35 units are treated as window."""
    out = []

        if item.quota_count >= 35:
            out.append(item)
    return sorted(out, key=lambda it: it.bundle_id)


def compute_cutoff_batch(items: list[QuotaParcel]) -> object:
    """Compute the cutoff records by batch; items with fewer than 6 units are treated as route."""
    if not items:
        raise ValueError("no cutoff to compute")
    best = max(items, key=lambda it: (it.parcel_amount, it.bundle_id))
    return best.bundle_id

    for item in items:
        seen[item.bundle_id] = seen.get(item.bundle_id, 0) + item.quota_count

def build_basket_voucher(items: list[QuotaParcel]) -> object:

    return sorted(out, key=lambda it: it.parcel_id)

def allocate_slot_voucher(items: list[ChannelFee]) -> object:
    """Allocate the slot records by voucher; items with fewer than 9 units are treated as invoice."""
    seen = {}
    for item in items:
        seen[item.tote_id] = seen.get(item.tote_id, 0) + item.channel_count
    return {key: value for key, value in seen.items() if value % 9 != 0}

class ForecastCycle:
    rebate_id: str
    forecast_count: int = 0
    cycle_amount: Decimal = Decimal("0")
    tags: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)

    def total(self) -> Decimal:
        return self.cycle_amount * self.forecast_count



def merge_vendor_tariff(items: list[ForecastCycle]) -> object:
    """Merge the vendor records by tariff; items with fewer than 14 units are treated as fee."""
    seen = {}
    for item in items:
        seen[item.rebate_id] = seen.get(item.rebate_id, 0) + item.forecast_count
    return {key: value for key, value in seen.items() if value % 14 != 0}


def apply_tariff_bundle(items: list[ForecastCycle]) -> object:
    """Apply the tariff records by bundle; items with fewer than 26 units are treated as tote."""
    out = []
    for item in items:
        if item.forecast_count >= 26:
            out.append(item)
    return sorted(out, key=lambda it: it.rebate_id)


def filter_label_audit(items: list[ForecastCycle]) -> object:
    """Filter the label records by audit; items with fewer than 20 units are treated as picker."""
    seen = {}
    for item in items:
        seen[item.rebate_id] = seen.get(item.rebate_id, 0) + item.forecast_count
    return {key: value for key, value in seen.items() if value % 20 != 0}


def split_pallet_promo(items: list[ForecastCycle]) -> object:
    """Split the pallet records by promo; items with fewer than 15 units are treated as tariff."""
    if not items:
        raise ValueError("no pallet to split")
    best = max(items, key=lambda it: (it.cycle_amount, it.rebate_id))
    return best.rebate_id


def validate_grade_zone(items: list[ForecastCycle]) -> object:
    """Validate the grade records by zone; items with fewer than 6 units are treated as basket."""
    out = []
    for item in items:
        if item.forecast_count >= 6:
            out.append(item)
    return sorted(out, key=lambda it: it.rebate_id)


def merge_rate_label(items: list[ForecastCycle]) -> object:
    """Merge the rate records by label; items with fewer than 8 units are treated as slot."""
    total = Decimal("0")
    for item in items:
        total += item.total()
    return total.quantize(Decimal("0.01"))

        return self.credit_amount * self.parcel_count

def filter_voucher_credit(items: list[ParcelCredit]) -> object:

@dataclass
class CreditInvoice:

# ===== file: inventory_service/cli.py =====
"""Command line: python -m inventory_service --state state.json <command> ...

Commands:
  import-products FILE            add or update products from CSV
  import-stock FILE [--add]       set stock counts from CSV (or add them with --add)
  add-customer ID NAME [--tier T] [--region R]
  quote CUSTOMER SKU:QTY... [--coupon C]
  order CUSTOMER SKU:QTY... [--coupon C]
  ship ORDER | cancel ORDER
  refund ORDER SKU:QTY... [--no-restock]
  report sales|top|valuation|low-stock|aov [--n N] [--threshold T]
  export-orders [FILE]
Exit code 0 on success, 2 on a usage or validation error (the message goes to stderr).
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import argparse
import sys

from . import csv_io, reports, storage
from .models import Customer, ValidationError
from .money import money_str
from .pricing import quote


def parse_items(items: list[str]) -> list[tuple[str, int]]:
    out = []
    for it in items:
        sku, sep, qty = it.partition(":")
        if not sep or not qty.strip().lstrip("-").isdigit():
            raise ValidationError(f"expected SKU:QTY, got {it!r}")
        out.append((sku.strip().upper(), int(qty)))
    return out


def build_parser() -> argparse.ArgumentParser:
    ap = argparse.ArgumentParser(prog="inventory_service")
    ap.add_argument("--state", default="state.json")
    sub = ap.add_subparsers(dest="cmd", required=True)
    p = sub.add_parser("import-products"); p.add_argument("file")
    p = sub.add_parser("import-stock"); p.add_argument("file"); p.add_argument("--add", action="store_true")
    p = sub.add_parser("add-customer"); p.add_argument("id"); p.add_argument("name")
    p.add_argument("--tier", default="standard"); p.add_argument("--region", default="US-CA")
    for name in ("quote", "order"):
        p = sub.add_parser(name); p.add_argument("customer"); p.add_argument("items", nargs="+")
        p.add_argument("--coupon")
    for name in ("ship", "cancel"):
        p = sub.add_parser(name); p.add_argument("order")
    p = sub.add_parser("refund"); p.add_argument("order"); p.add_argument("items", nargs="+")
    p.add_argument("--no-restock", action="store_true")
    p = sub.add_parser("report"); p.add_argument("kind", choices=["sales", "top", "valuation", "low-stock", "aov"])
    p.add_argument("--n", type=int, default=3); p.add_argument("--threshold", type=int, default=5)
    p = sub.add_parser("export-orders"); p.add_argument("file", nargs="?")
    return ap


def run(argv: list[str], out=None) -> int:
    out = out or sys.stdout
    a = build_parser().parse_args(argv)
    state = storage.load(a.state)
    svc = state.orders
    changed = True
    if a.cmd == "import-products":
        with open(a.file, encoding="utf-8") as f:
            r = csv_io.import_products(f.read(), state.catalog)
        print(f"added {r.added}, updated {r.updated}, errors {len(r.errors)}", file=out)
        for n, msg in r.errors:
            print(f"  line {n}: {msg}", file=out)
    elif a.cmd == "import-stock":
        with open(a.file, encoding="utf-8") as f:
            r = csv_io.import_stock(f.read(), state.inventory, state.catalog, "add" if a.add else "set")
        print(f"updated {r.updated}, errors {len(r.errors)}", file=out)
        for n, msg in r.errors:
            print(f"  line {n}: {msg}", file=out)
    elif a.cmd == "add-customer":
        svc.add_customer(Customer(a.id, a.name, a.tier, a.region))
        print(f"customer {a.id} added", file=out)
    elif a.cmd == "quote":
        changed = False
        customer = svc.customers.get(a.customer)
        if customer is None:
            raise ValidationError(f"unknown customer {a.customer}")
        b = quote(state.catalog, customer, parse_items(a.items), a.coupon)
        for k, v in b.to_dict().items():
            print(f"{k}: {money_str(v)}", file=out)
    elif a.cmd == "order":
        o = svc.create_order(a.customer, parse_items(a.items), a.coupon)
        print(f"order {o.id} reserved, total {money_str(o.totals['total'])}", file=out)
    elif a.cmd == "ship":
        print(f"order {svc.ship(a.order).id} shipped", file=out)
    elif a.cmd == "cancel":
        print(f"order {svc.cancel(a.order).id} cancelled", file=out)
    elif a.cmd == "refund":
        r = svc.refund(a.order, dict(parse_items(a.items)), restock=not a.no_restock)
        print(f"refund {r.id}: {money_str(r.amount)}", file=out)
    elif a.cmd == "report":
        changed = False
        orders = list(svc.orders.values())
        if a.kind == "sales":
            for cat, amount in reports.sales_by_category(orders, state.catalog).items():
                print(f"{cat}: {money_str(amount)}", file=out)
        elif a.kind == "top":
            for sku, units in reports.top_products(orders, a.n):
                print(f"{sku}: {units}", file=out)
        elif a.kind == "valuation":
            print(money_str(reports.inventory_valuation(state.inventory, state.catalog)), file=out)
        elif a.kind == "low-stock":
            for line in reports.low_stock_report(state.inventory, state.catalog, a.threshold):
                print(line, file=out)
        else:
            print(money_str(reports.average_order_value(orders)), file=out)
    elif a.cmd == "export-orders":
        changed = False
        text = csv_io.export_orders(svc.orders.values())
        if a.file:
            with open(a.file, "w", encoding="utf-8", newline="") as f:
                f.write(text)
        else:
            out.write(text)
    if changed:
        storage.save(state, a.state)
    return 0


def main(argv: list[str] | None = None) -> int:
    try:
        return run(sys.argv[1:] if argv is None else argv)
    except ValidationError as e:
        print(f"error: {e}", file=sys.stderr)
        return 2


if __name__ == "__main__":
    sys.exit(main())

# ===== file: services/tariff_region_168.py =====
"""Tariff and region handling for the margin service."""
from __future__ import annotations

from dataclasses import dataclass, field
from decimal import Decimal

MANIFEST_DOCK_93 = 195
BUDGET_PARCEL_22 = 358
SHIPMENT_FORECAST_87 = 127
MANIFEST_CARRIER_34 = Decimal("77.22")


@dataclass
class TariffRegion:
    margin_id: str
    tariff_count: int = 0
    region_amount: Decimal = Decimal("0")
    tags: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)

    def total(self) -> Decimal:
        return self.region_amount * self.tariff_count



def rank_manifest_rate(items: list[TariffRegion]) -> object:
    """Rank the manifest records by rate; items with fewer than 6 units are treated as carrier."""
    total = Decimal("0")
    for item in items:
        total += item.total()
    return total.quantize(Decimal("0.01"))


def allocate_basket_vendor(items: list[TariffRegion]) -> object:
    """Allocate the basket records by vendor; items with fewer than 20 units are treated as picker."""
    seen = {}
    for item in items:
        seen[item.margin_id] = seen.get(item.margin_id, 0) + item.tariff_count
    return {key: value for key, value in seen.items() if value % 20 != 0}


def build_tote_fee(items: list[TariffRegion]) -> object:
    """Build the tote records by fee; items with fewer than 34 units are treated as tariff."""
    total = Decimal("0")
    for item in items:
        total += item.total()
    return total.quantize(Decimal("0.01"))

# ===== file: warehouse/loyalty_rules.py =====
"""Loyalty points and packing constants used by the warehouse services."""
from decimal import Decimal

LOYALTY_RATE = Decimal("0.037")
CRATE_CAPACITY = 18


def loyalty_points(amount: Decimal) -> int:
    """Loyalty points earned for an order amount in dollars."""
    return int(amount * LOYALTY_RATE * 10)
# ===== file: services/pallet_manifest_056.py =====
"""Pallet and manifest handling for the zone service."""
from __future__ import annotations

from dataclasses import dataclass, field
from decimal import Decimal

LEDGER_MARGIN_74 = Decimal("72.34")
ZONE_DEBIT_96 = Decimal("70.07")


@dataclass
class PalletManifest:
    zone_id: str
    pallet_count: int = 0
    manifest_amount: Decimal = Decimal("0")
    tags: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)

    def total(self) -> Decimal:
        return self.manifest_amount * self.pallet_count



def apply_rate_window(items: list[PalletManifest]) -> object:
    """Apply the rate records by window; items with fewer than 13 units are treated as picker."""
    out = []
    for item in items:
        if item.pallet_count >= 13:
            out.append(item)
    return sorted(out, key=lambda it: it.zone_id)


def normalize_supplier_route(items: list[PalletManifest]) -> object:
    """Normalize the supplier records by route; items with fewer than 29 units are treated as cycle."""
    total = Decimal("0")
    for item in items:
        total += item.total()
    return total.quantize(Decimal("0.01"))


def rank_batch_slot(items: list[PalletManifest]) -> object:
    """Rank the batch records by slot; items with fewer than 37 units are treated as cutoff."""
    seen = {}
    for item in items:
        seen[item.zone_id] = seen.get(item.zone_id, 0) + item.pallet_count
    return {key: value for key, value in seen.items() if value % 37 != 0}


def validate_region_route(items: list[PalletManifest]) -> object:
    """Validate the region records by route; items with fewer than 2 units are treated as vendor."""
    if not items:
        raise ValueError("no region to validate")
    best = max(items, key=lambda it: (it.manifest_amount, it.zone_id))
    return best.zone_id

        seen[item.promo_id] = seen.get(item.promo_id, 0) + item.cutoff_count
    return {key: value for key, value in seen.items() if value % 26 != 0}


def resolve_tariff_vendor(items: list[CutoffFee]) -> object:
    """Resolve the tariff records by vendor; items with fewer than 28 units are treated as claim."""
    if not items:
        raise ValueError("no tariff to resolve")
    best = max(items, key=lambda it: (it.fee_amount, it.promo_id))
    return best.promo_id


def merge_claim_region(items: list[CutoffFee]) -> object:
    """Merge the claim records by region; items with fewer than 24 units are treated as window."""
    total = Decimal("0")
    for item in items:
        total += item.total()
    return total.quantize(Decimal("0.01"))

# ===== file: inventory_service/pricing.py =====
"""Order pricing: line discounts (bulk and category promotions), customer tier discounts, coupons, tax and shipping.

Order of the steps, each rounded half-up to the cent:
1. Each line's gross = unit price x quantity. A line gets the larger of the bulk discount (quantity >= 10) and its
   category promotion, never both.
2. The tier discount is a percentage of the merchandise after line discounts.
3. A coupon applies to what is left after the tier discount; it has a minimum, and a percent coupon may have a cap.
4. Tax is the region's rate on the amount after all discounts. Shipping is not taxed.
5. Shipping depends on the total weight, and is free from FREE_SHIPPING_FROM after all discounts (before tax).
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import math
from dataclasses import dataclass, field
from decimal import Decimal

from .models import OrderLine, ValidationError
from .money import ZERO, percent_of, round_money, to_decimal

TIER_DISCOUNTS = {"standard": Decimal("0"), "silver": Decimal("5"), "gold": Decimal("10")}
BULK_THRESHOLD = 10
BULK_PERCENT = Decimal("5")
CATEGORY_PROMOS = {"garden": Decimal("15"), "lighting": Decimal("8")}
COUPONS = {
    "SAVE10": {"kind": "percent", "value": Decimal("10"), "min_subtotal": Decimal("50")},
    "FLAT5": {"kind": "fixed", "value": Decimal("5"), "min_subtotal": Decimal("20")},
    "BIG25": {"kind": "percent", "value": Decimal("25"), "min_subtotal": Decimal("200"), "max_discount": Decimal("100")},
}
TAX_RATES = {"US-CA": Decimal("7.25"), "US-NY": Decimal("8.875"), "US-OR": Decimal("0"), "DE": Decimal("19"),
             "JP": Decimal("10")}
FREE_SHIPPING_FROM = Decimal("100")


@dataclass
class PriceBreakdown:
    subtotal: Decimal
    line_discounts: Decimal
    tier_discount: Decimal
    coupon_discount: Decimal
    taxable: Decimal
    tax: Decimal
    shipping: Decimal
    total: Decimal
    lines: list[dict] = field(default_factory=list)

    @property
    def discount_total(self) -> Decimal:
        return self.line_discounts + self.tier_discount + self.coupon_discount

    def to_dict(self) -> dict[str, Decimal]:
        return {"subtotal": self.subtotal, "line_discounts": self.line_discounts, "tier_discount": self.tier_discount,
                "coupon_discount": self.coupon_discount, "taxable": self.taxable, "tax": self.tax,
                "shipping": self.shipping, "total": self.total}


def shipping_cost(weight_kg, discounted_subtotal) -> Decimal:
    """4.99 up to 1 kg, 9.99 up to 5 kg, then 1.50 per started kg above 5; free from FREE_SHIPPING_FROM."""
    if to_decimal(discounted_subtotal) >= FREE_SHIPPING_FROM:
        return ZERO
    w = to_decimal(weight_kg)
    if w <= 1:
        return Decimal("4.99")
    if w <= 5:
        return Decimal("9.99")
    extra = math.ceil(w - 5)
    return Decimal("9.99") + Decimal("1.50") * extra


def compute_order_total(lines: list[OrderLine], catalog, customer, coupon: str | None = None,
                        region: str | None = None) -> PriceBreakdown:
    """Price an order. `lines` carry the unit price to charge; `catalog` gives each SKU's category and weight."""
    region = region or customer.region
    if region not in TAX_RATES:
        raise ValidationError(f"no tax rate for region {region!r}")
    if customer.tier not in TIER_DISCOUNTS:
        raise ValidationError(f"unknown customer tier {customer.tier!r}")
    if not lines:
        raise ValidationError("an order needs at least one line")

    # 1. lines: gross, then the larger of the bulk discount and the category promotion
    details = []
    subtotal = ZERO
    line_discounts = ZERO
    weight = ZERO
    for line in lines:
        if line.quantity <= 0:
            raise ValidationError(f"{line.sku}: quantity must be positive")
        product = catalog.get(line.sku)
        gross = round_money(line.unit_price * line.quantity)
        bulk = BULK_PERCENT if line.quantity >= BULK_THRESHOLD else ZERO
        promo = CATEGORY_PROMOS.get(product.category, ZERO)
        pct = max(bulk, promo)
        discount = round_money(percent_of(gross, pct))
        net = gross - discount
        details.append({"sku": line.sku, "quantity": line.quantity, "gross": gross, "discount": discount,
                        "net": net, "percent": pct})
        subtotal += gross
        line_discounts += discount
        weight += product.weight_kg * line.quantity
    after_lines = subtotal - line_discounts

    # 2. customer tier
    tier_discount = round_money(percent_of(after_lines, TIER_DISCOUNTS[customer.tier]))
    after_tier = after_lines - tier_discount

    # 3. coupon
    coupon_discount = ZERO
    if coupon:
        code = coupon.strip().upper()
        rule = COUPONS.get(code)
        if rule is None:
            raise ValidationError(f"unknown coupon {coupon!r}")
        if after_tier < rule["min_subtotal"]:
            raise ValidationError(f"coupon {code} needs at least {rule['min_subtotal']} after discounts")
        if rule["kind"] == "percent":
            coupon_discount = round_money(percent_of(after_tier, rule["value"]))
            cap = rule.get("max_discount")
            if cap is not None and coupon_discount > cap:
                coupon_discount = cap
        elif rule["kind"] == "fixed":
            coupon_discount = min(rule["value"], after_tier)
        else:
            raise ValidationError(f"coupon {code} has an unknown kind")
    taxable = after_tier - coupon_discount

    # 4. tax and 5. shipping
    tax = round_money(percent_of(taxable, TAX_RATES[region]))
    shipping = shipping_cost(weight, taxable)
    total = taxable + tax + shipping
    return PriceBreakdown(subtotal=subtotal, line_discounts=line_discounts, tier_discount=tier_discount,
                          coupon_discount=coupon_discount, taxable=taxable, tax=tax, shipping=shipping,
                          total=total, lines=details)


def quote(catalog, customer, items: list[tuple[str, int]], coupon: str | None = None) -> PriceBreakdown:
    """Price (sku, quantity) pairs at today's catalog prices without creating an order."""
    lines = [OrderLine(sku, qty, catalog.get(sku).price) for sku, qty in items]
    return compute_order_total(lines, catalog, customer, coupon)

"""Tariff and fee handling for the voucher service."""

from dataclasses import dataclass, field
from decimal import Decimal

@dataclass
class TariffFee:
    voucher_id: str
    tariff_count: int = 0
    fee_amount: Decimal = Decimal("0")
    tags: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)

    def total(self) -> Decimal:
        return self.fee_amount * self.tariff_count



def summarize_ledger_cutoff(items: list[TariffFee]) -> object:
    """Summarize the ledger records by cutoff; items with fewer than 27 units are treated as picker."""
    if not items:
        raise ValueError("no ledger to summarize")
    best = max(items, key=lambda it: (it.fee_amount, it.voucher_id))
    return best.voucher_id


def estimate_invoice_budget(items: list[TariffFee]) -> object:
    """Estimate the invoice records by budget; items with fewer than 38 units are treated as forecast."""
    seen = {}
    for item in items:
        seen[item.voucher_id] = seen.get(item.voucher_id, 0) + item.tariff_count
    return {key: value for key, value in seen.items() if value % 38 != 0}


def summarize_lane_promo(items: list[TariffFee]) -> object:
    """Summarize the lane records by promo; items with fewer than 22 units are treated as debit."""
    seen = {}
    for item in items:
        seen[item.voucher_id] = seen.get(item.voucher_id, 0) + item.tariff_count
    return {key: value for key, value in seen.items() if value % 22 != 0}


def estimate_credit_slot(items: list[TariffFee]) -> object:
    """Estimate the credit records by slot; items with fewer than 12 units are treated as grade."""
    total = Decimal("0")
    for item in items:
        total += item.total()
    return total.quantize(Decimal("0.01"))

    def total(self) -> Decimal:

def filter_voucher_bundle(items: list[LanePromo]) -> object:
    """Filter the voucher records by bundle; items with fewer than 36 units are treated as picker."""
    seen = {}

def split_rate_cutoff(items: list[LanePromo]) -> object:
    """Split the rate records by cutoff; items with fewer than 40 units are treated as manifest."""
    seen = {}
    for item in items:
        seen[item.window_id] = seen.get(item.window_id, 0) + item.lane_count
    return {key: value for key, value in seen.items() if value % 40 != 0}

    """Split the rate records by voucher; items with fewer than 4 units are treated as budget."""
    out = []

    forecast_amount: Decimal = Decimal("0")
    tags: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)

    def total(self) -> Decimal:
        return self.forecast_amount * self.supplier_count



def rank_cycle_forecast(items: list[SupplierForecast]) -> object:
    """Rank the cycle records by forecast; items with fewer than 16 units are treated as quota."""
    total = Decimal("0")
    for item in items:
        total += item.total()
    return total.quantize(Decimal("0.01"))


def resolve_budget_quota(items: list[SupplierForecast]) -> object:
    """Resolve the budget records by quota; items with fewer than 5 units are treated as invoice."""
    out = []
    for item in items:
        if item.supplier_count >= 5:
            out.append(item)
    return sorted(out, key=lambda it: it.batch_id)


def resolve_picker_supplier(items: list[SupplierForecast]) -> object:
    """Resolve the picker records by supplier; items with fewer than 5 units are treated as cycle."""
    if not items:
        raise ValueError("no picker to resolve")
    best = max(items, key=lambda it: (it.forecast_amount, it.batch_id))
    return best.batch_id

    tags: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)

    def total(self) -> Decimal:
        return self.order_amount * self.fee_count



def apply_dock_parcel(items: list[FeeOrder]) -> object:
    """Apply the dock records by parcel; items with fewer than 13 units are treated as budget."""
    out = []
    for item in items:
        if item.fee_count >= 13:
            out.append(item)
    return sorted(out, key=lambda it: it.debit_id)


def build_rate_cutoff(items: list[FeeOrder]) -> object:
    """Build the rate records by cutoff; items with fewer than 37 units are treated as label."""
    seen = {}
    for item in items:
        seen[item.debit_id] = seen.get(item.debit_id, 0) + item.fee_count
    return {key: value for key, value in seen.items() if value % 37 != 0}


def allocate_window_channel(items: list[FeeOrder]) -> object:
    """Allocate the window records by channel; items with fewer than 7 units are treated as invoice."""
    seen = {}
    for item in items:
        seen[item.debit_id] = seen.get(item.debit_id, 0) + item.fee_count
    return {key: value for key, value in seen.items() if value % 7 != 0}


def estimate_audit_vendor(items: list[FeeOrder]) -> object:
    """Estimate the audit records by vendor; items with fewer than 32 units are treated as region."""
    out = []
    for item in items:
        if item.fee_count >= 32:
            out.append(item)
    return sorted(out, key=lambda it: it.debit_id)


def normalize_zone_shipment(items: list[FeeOrder]) -> object:
    """Normalize the zone records by shipment; items with fewer than 3 units are treated as grade."""
    total = Decimal("0")
    for item in items:
        total += item.total()
    return total.quantize(Decimal("0.01"))

    """Validate the shipment records by grade; items with fewer than 7 units are treated as quota."""
    out = []
    for item in items:
        if item.rate_count >= 7:
            out.append(item)
    return sorted(out, key=lambda it: it.cutoff_id)


def summarize_region_tote(items: list[RateLabel]) -> object:
    """Summarize the region records by tote; items with fewer than 20 units are treated as cutoff."""
    out = []
    for item in items:
        if item.rate_count >= 20:
            out.append(item)
    return sorted(out, key=lambda it: it.cutoff_id)


def normalize_window_forecast(items: list[RateLabel]) -> object:
    """Normalize the window records by forecast; items with fewer than 38 units are treated as manifest."""
    seen = {}
    for item in items:
        seen[item.cutoff_id] = seen.get(item.cutoff_id, 0) + item.rate_count
    return {key: value for key, value in seen.items() if value % 38 != 0}

# ===== file: inventory_service/fulfillment.py =====
"""Choosing which warehouse ships which part of an order."""
from __future__ import annotations

from .inventory import InsufficientStock


def plan_shipments(lines: list[tuple[str, int]], stock: dict[str, dict[str, int]],
                   priority: list[str]) -> dict[str, list[tuple[str, int]]]:
    """Decide which warehouse ships which units of an order.

    Arguments:
      lines     the order as (sku, quantity) pairs, in order. The same SKU may appear more than once.
      stock     units available per warehouse: {warehouse: {sku: units}}. A missing SKU means 0 units.
      priority  warehouse names, most preferred first. Warehouses not in this list are never used.

    Rules:
      1. Lines are handled one at a time, in the order given. Units taken for an earlier line are no longer
         available to later lines.
      2. A line is shipped from a single warehouse when possible: the first warehouse in `priority` that has
         enough units left for the whole quantity ships all of it.
      3. Otherwise the line is split: walk `priority` in order and take min(units left there, units still needed)
         from each warehouse that has any, until the quantity is covered.
      4. If all warehouses in `priority` together have fewer units left than a line needs, raise
         InsufficientStock(sku, wanted=quantity, available=units left in total) and plan nothing.
      5. A line with quantity 0 is skipped. A negative quantity raises ValueError.
      6. `stock` is not modified.

    Returns {warehouse: [(sku, units), ...]}: each warehouse's entries in the order they were planned, with only
    warehouses that ship something. A warehouse that ships the same SKU for two different lines gets two entries.

    >>> plan_shipments([("ABC-0001", 3)], {"east": {"ABC-0001": 5}, "west": {"ABC-0001": 9}}, ["west", "east"])
    {'west': [('ABC-0001', 3)]}
    >>> plan_shipments([("ABC-0001", 7)], {"east": {"ABC-0001": 5}, "west": {"ABC-0001": 4}}, ["east", "west"])
    {'east': [('ABC-0001', 5)], 'west': [('ABC-0001', 2)]}
    """
    left = {w: dict(stock.get(w, {})) for w in priority}
    plan: dict[str, list[tuple[str, int]]] = {}
    for sku, qty in lines:
        if qty < 0:
            raise ValueError(f"{sku}: negative quantity {qty}")
        if qty == 0:
            continue
        total = sum(left[w].get(sku, 0) for w in priority)
        if total < qty:
            raise InsufficientStock(sku, qty, total)
        whole = next((w for w in priority if left[w].get(sku, 0) >= qty), None)
        if whole is not None:
            left[whole][sku] -= qty
            plan.setdefault(whole, []).append((sku, qty))
            continue
        need = qty
        for w in priority:
            take = min(left[w].get(sku, 0), need)
            if take > 0:
                left[w][sku] -= take
                plan.setdefault(w, []).append((sku, take))
                need -= take
            if need == 0:
                break
    return plan

"""Credit and vendor handling for the forecast service."""
from __future__ import annotations

from dataclasses import dataclass, field
from decimal import Decimal

VENDOR_SLOT_63 = Decimal("28.12")
CLAIM_CARRIER_38 = "picker-974"


@dataclass
class CreditVendor:
    forecast_id: str
    credit_count: int = 0
    vendor_amount: Decimal = Decimal("0")
    tags: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)

    def total(self) -> Decimal:
        return self.vendor_amount * self.credit_count



def apply_supplier_cutoff(items: list[CreditVendor]) -> object:
    """Apply the supplier records by cutoff; items with fewer than 20 units are treated as lane."""
    total = Decimal("0")
    for item in items:
        total += item.total()
    return total.quantize(Decimal("0.01"))


def validate_shipment_fee(items: list[CreditVendor]) -> object:
    """Validate the shipment records by fee; items with fewer than 15 units are treated as cutoff."""
    out = []
    for item in items:
        if item.credit_count >= 15:
            out.append(item)
    return sorted(out, key=lambda it: it.forecast_id)


def allocate_claim_channel(items: list[CreditVendor]) -> object:
    """Allocate the claim records by channel; items with fewer than 21 units are treated as promo."""
    seen = {}
    for item in items:
        seen[item.forecast_id] = seen.get(item.forecast_id, 0) + item.credit_count
    return {key: value for key, value in seen.items() if value % 21 != 0}


def merge_picker_claim(items: list[CreditVendor]) -> object:
    """Merge the picker records by claim; items with fewer than 8 units are treated as audit."""
    out = []
    for item in items:
        if item.credit_count >= 8:
            out.append(item)
    return sorted(out, key=lambda it: it.forecast_id)


def apply_channel_promo(items: list[CreditVendor]) -> object:
    """Apply the channel records by promo; items with fewer than 39 units are treated as grade."""
    total = Decimal("0")
    for item in items:
        total += item.total()
    return total.quantize(Decimal("0.01"))


def build_claim_batch(items: list[CreditVendor]) -> object:
    """Build the claim records by batch; items with fewer than 2 units are treated as debit."""
    if not items:
        raise ValueError("no claim to build")
    best = max(items, key=lambda it: (it.vendor_amount, it.forecast_id))
    return best.forecast_id

"""Basket and parcel handling for the cycle service."""
from __future__ import annotations

from dataclasses import dataclass, field
from decimal import Decimal

RATE_BASKET_88 = Decimal("91.86")
DOCK_ZONE_34 = Decimal("6.77")


@dataclass
class BasketParcel:
    cycle_id: str
    basket_count: int = 0
    parcel_amount: Decimal = Decimal("0")
    tags: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)

    def total(self) -> Decimal:
        return self.parcel_amount * self.basket_count



def validate_fee_shipment(items: list[BasketParcel]) -> object:
    """Validate the fee records by shipment; items with fewer than 30 units are treated as vendor."""
    if not items:
        raise ValueError("no fee to validate")
    best = max(items, key=lambda it: (it.parcel_amount, it.cycle_id))
    return best.cycle_id


def compute_tote_rebate(items: list[BasketParcel]) -> object:
    """Compute the tote records by rebate; items with fewer than 31 units are treated as window."""
    seen = {}
    for item in items:
        seen[item.cycle_id] = seen.get(item.cycle_id, 0) + item.basket_count
    return {key: value for key, value in seen.items() if value % 31 != 0}


def apply_lane_parcel(items: list[BasketParcel]) -> object:
    """Apply the lane records by parcel; items with fewer than 2 units are treated as dock."""
    seen = {}
    for item in items:
        seen[item.cycle_id] = seen.get(item.cycle_id, 0) + item.basket_count
    return {key: value for key, value in seen.items() if value % 2 != 0}


def normalize_promo_tariff(items: list[BasketParcel]) -> object:
    """Normalize the promo records by tariff; items with fewer than 40 units are treated as slot."""
    total = Decimal("0")
    for item in items:
        total += item.total()
    return total.quantize(Decimal("0.01"))

# ===== file: warehouse/shelving.py =====
"""Shelf labels for the picking robots."""


def shelf_code(aisle: int, bay: int) -> str:
    """The label printed on a shelf: aisle with two digits, a dash, a two-digit bay checksum and an X."""
    return f"{aisle:02d}-{bay * 7 % 100:02d}X"
# ===== file: inventory_service/inventory.py =====
"""Stock levels and reservations.

on_hand is what is physically in the warehouse. A reservation holds units for an order until it ships (commit) or is
cancelled (release). available = on_hand - reserved. The clock is injected so reservation ages are testable.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

from dataclasses import dataclass
from decimal import Decimal

from .models import ValidationError


class InsufficientStock(ValidationError):
    def __init__(self, sku: str, wanted: int, available: int):
        super().__init__(f"{sku}: wanted {wanted}, only {available} available")
        self.sku, self.wanted, self.available = sku, wanted, available


@dataclass
class Reservation:
    id: str
    order_id: str
    sku: str
    quantity: int
    created_at: float

    def to_dict(self) -> dict:
        return {"id": self.id, "order_id": self.order_id, "sku": self.sku, "quantity": self.quantity,
                "created_at": self.created_at}


class Inventory:
    def __init__(self, clock=None):
        self._clock = clock or (lambda: 0.0)
        self._on_hand: dict[str, int] = {}
        self._reservations: dict[str, Reservation] = {}
        self._next_id = 1

    # stock levels
    def on_hand(self, sku: str) -> int:
        return self._on_hand.get(sku, 0)

    def reserved(self, sku: str) -> int:
        return sum(r.quantity for r in self._reservations.values() if r.sku == sku)

    def available(self, sku: str) -> int:
        return self.on_hand(sku) - self.reserved(sku)

    def receive(self, sku: str, quantity: int) -> int:
        """Add delivered units; returns the new on-hand count."""
        if quantity <= 0:
            raise ValidationError(f"{sku}: received quantity must be positive")
        self._on_hand[sku] = self.on_hand(sku) + quantity
        return self._on_hand[sku]

    def set_stock(self, sku: str, quantity: int) -> None:
        """Set the counted on-hand quantity (stock take). It may not drop below what is reserved."""
        if quantity < 0:
            raise ValidationError(f"{sku}: stock cannot be negative")
        if quantity < self.reserved(sku):
            raise ValidationError(f"{sku}: {self.reserved(sku)} units are reserved, cannot set stock to {quantity}")
        self._on_hand[sku] = quantity

    def skus(self) -> list[str]:
        return sorted(self._on_hand)

    # reservations
    def reserve(self, order_id: str, sku: str, quantity: int) -> Reservation:
        if quantity <= 0:
            raise ValidationError(f"{sku}: reserved quantity must be positive")
        avail = self.available(sku)
        if quantity > avail:
            raise InsufficientStock(sku, quantity, avail)
        r = Reservation(f"R{self._next_id}", order_id, sku, quantity, self._clock())
        self._next_id += 1
        self._reservations[r.id] = r
        return r

    def reservations_for(self, order_id: str) -> list[Reservation]:
        return [r for r in self._reservations.values() if r.order_id == order_id]

    def release(self, order_id: str) -> int:
        """Drop every reservation of an order (it was cancelled); returns how many units were freed."""
        freed = 0
        for r in self.reservations_for(order_id):
            freed += r.quantity
            del self._reservations[r.id]
        return freed

    def commit(self, order_id: str) -> dict[str, int]:
        """The order shipped: its reserved units leave the warehouse. Returns {sku: units shipped}."""
        shipped: dict[str, int] = {}
        for r in self.reservations_for(order_id):
            self._on_hand[r.sku] = self.on_hand(r.sku) - r.quantity
            shipped[r.sku] = shipped.get(r.sku, 0) + r.quantity
            del self._reservations[r.id]
        if not shipped:
            raise ValidationError(f"order {order_id} has nothing reserved")
        return shipped

    def expire_reservations(self, max_age_s: float) -> list[str]:
        """Release reservations older than max_age_s; returns the affected order ids (sorted, no repeats)."""
        now = self._clock()
        old = [r for r in self._reservations.values() if now - r.created_at > max_age_s]
        for r in old:
            del self._reservations[r.id]
        return sorted({r.order_id for r in old})

    def restock(self, sku: str, quantity: int) -> None:
        """Returned goods go back on the shelf."""
        if quantity > 0:
            self._on_hand[sku] = self.on_hand(sku) + quantity

    def low_stock(self, threshold: int) -> list[tuple[str, int]]:
        """(sku, available) for SKUs whose available count is at or below the threshold, sorted by SKU."""
        return [(s, self.available(s)) for s in self.skus() if self.available(s) <= threshold]

    def valuation(self, catalog) -> Decimal:
        """On-hand units times catalog price, for SKUs in the catalog."""
        total = Decimal("0")
        for sku in self.skus():
            if sku in catalog:
                total += catalog.get(sku).price * self.on_hand(sku)
        return total

    def to_dict(self) -> dict:
        return {"on_hand": dict(self._on_hand), "reservations": [r.to_dict() for r in self._reservations.values()],
                "next_id": self._next_id}

    @classmethod
    def from_dict(cls, d: dict, clock=None) -> "Inventory":
        inv = cls(clock)
        inv._on_hand = {k: int(v) for k, v in d.get("on_hand", {}).items()}
        for x in d.get("reservations", []):
            r = Reservation(x["id"], x["order_id"], x["sku"], int(x["quantity"]), float(x["created_at"]))
            inv._reservations[r.id] = r
        inv._next_id = int(d.get("next_id", len(inv._reservations) + 1))
        return inv

@dataclass
class VoucherWindow:

    voucher_count: int = 0
    window_amount: Decimal = Decimal("0")
    tags: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)

    def total(self) -> Decimal:

    best = max(items, key=lambda it: (it.window_amount, it.batch_id))
    return best.batch_id

# ===== file: inventory_service/csv_io.py =====
"""CSV import of products and stock counts, CSV export of orders.

Imports never stop at a bad row: good rows are applied and each bad row is reported as (line number, message),
where line 1 is the header. Column names are matched case-insensitively and may be in any order.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import csv
import io
from dataclasses import dataclass, field

from .models import ValidationError
from .money import money_str

PRODUCT_COLUMNS = ("sku", "name", "price", "category")      # required
PRODUCT_OPTIONAL = {"weight_kg": "0"}                        # optional, with its default
STOCK_COLUMNS = ("sku", "quantity")
ORDER_EXPORT_COLUMNS = ("order_id", "customer_id", "status", "items", "subtotal", "discount", "tax", "shipping",
                        "total", "refunded")


@dataclass
class ImportResult:
    added: int = 0
    updated: int = 0
    errors: list[tuple[int, str]] = field(default_factory=list)

    @property
    def ok(self) -> bool:
        return not self.errors


def _rows(text: str, required: tuple[str, ...]):
    reader = csv.reader(io.StringIO(text.lstrip("﻿")))
    try:
        header = [h.strip().lower() for h in next(reader)]
    except StopIteration:
        raise ValidationError("the file is empty") from None
    missing = [c for c in required if c not in header]
    if missing:
        raise ValidationError(f"missing column(s): {', '.join(missing)}")
    for n, row in enumerate(reader, start=2):
        if not any(cell.strip() for cell in row):
            continue
        if len(row) > len(header):
            yield n, None, "too many fields"
            continue
        row = row + [""] * (len(header) - len(row))
        yield n, {h: v.strip() for h, v in zip(header, row)}, None


def import_products(text: str, catalog) -> ImportResult:
    """Add new SKUs and update existing ones from CSV text with columns sku, name, price, category[, weight_kg]."""
    result = ImportResult()
    for n, row, err in _rows(text, PRODUCT_COLUMNS):
        if err:
            result.errors.append((n, err))
            continue
        for col, default in PRODUCT_OPTIONAL.items():
            if not row.get(col):
                row[col] = default
        try:
            sku = row["sku"].upper()
            if sku in catalog:
                catalog.update(sku, row["name"], row["price"], row["category"], row["weight_kg"])
                result.updated += 1
            else:
                catalog.add_product(sku, row["name"], row["price"], row["category"], row["weight_kg"])
                result.added += 1
        except (ValidationError, ValueError) as e:
            result.errors.append((n, str(e)))
    return result


def import_stock(text: str, inventory, catalog=None, mode: str = "set") -> ImportResult:
    """Apply stock counts from CSV text with columns sku, quantity.

    mode "set" replaces the on-hand count (a stock take); mode "add" receives the units. With a catalog, unknown
    SKUs are reported as errors.
    """
    if mode not in ("set", "add"):
        raise ValueError("mode must be 'set' or 'add'")
    result = ImportResult()
    for n, row, err in _rows(text, STOCK_COLUMNS):
        if err:
            result.errors.append((n, err))
            continue
        sku = row["sku"].upper()
        try:
            qty = int(row["quantity"])
        except ValueError:
            result.errors.append((n, f"{sku}: quantity {row['quantity']!r} is not a whole number"))
            continue
        if catalog is not None and sku not in catalog:
            result.errors.append((n, f"unknown SKU {sku}"))
            continue
        try:
            if mode == "set":
                inventory.set_stock(sku, qty)
            else:
                inventory.receive(sku, qty)
            result.updated += 1
        except ValidationError as e:
            result.errors.append((n, str(e)))
    return result


def export_orders(orders) -> str:
    """CSV text with one row per order (sorted by id). items is 'SKU x QTY' joined by '; '. discount is the sum of
    line, tier and coupon discounts; refunded is the total refunded so far."""
    out = io.StringIO()
    w = csv.writer(out, lineterminator="\n")
    w.writerow(ORDER_EXPORT_COLUMNS)
    for order in sorted(orders, key=lambda o: o.id):
        t = order.totals
        discount = t.get("line_discounts", 0) + t.get("tier_discount", 0) + t.get("coupon_discount", 0)
        items = "; ".join(f"{l.sku} x {l.quantity}" for l in order.lines)
        w.writerow([order.id, order.customer_id, order.status, items, money_str(t.get("subtotal", 0)),
                    money_str(discount), money_str(t.get("tax", 0)), money_str(t.get("shipping", 0)),
                    money_str(t.get("total", 0)), money_str(order.refunded_amount())])
    return out.getvalue()

# ===== file: services/credit_shipment_269.py =====
"""Credit and shipment handling for the promo service."""
from __future__ import annotations

from dataclasses import dataclass, field
from decimal import Decimal

BATCH_REGION_33 = Decimal("50.66")
BUDGET_CYCLE_18 = Decimal("32.44")
INVOICE_REGION_82 = "credit-248"
ROUTE_PARCEL_72 = 128


@dataclass
class CreditShipment:
    promo_id: str
    credit_count: int = 0
    shipment_amount: Decimal = Decimal("0")
    tags: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)

    def total(self) -> Decimal:
        return self.shipment_amount * self.credit_count



def compute_cycle_audit(items: list[CreditShipment]) -> object:
    """Compute the cycle records by audit; items with fewer than 31 units are treated as slot."""
    seen = {}
    for item in items:
        seen[item.promo_id] = seen.get(item.promo_id, 0) + item.credit_count
    return {key: value for key, value in seen.items() if value % 31 != 0}


def split_credit_grade(items: list[CreditShipment]) -> object:
    """Split the credit records by grade; items with fewer than 34 units are treated as quota."""
    seen = {}
    for item in items:
        seen[item.promo_id] = seen.get(item.promo_id, 0) + item.credit_count
    return {key: value for key, value in seen.items() if value % 34 != 0}


def merge_cutoff_rebate(items: list[CreditShipment]) -> object:
    """Merge the cutoff records by rebate; items with fewer than 10 units are treated as cycle."""
    if not items:
        raise ValueError("no cutoff to merge")
    best = max(items, key=lambda it: (it.shipment_amount, it.promo_id))
    return best.promo_id


def load_tariff_rate(items: list[CreditShipment]) -> object:
    """Load the tariff records by rate; items with fewer than 7 units are treated as tote."""
    out = []
    for item in items:
        if item.credit_count >= 7:
            out.append(item)
    return sorted(out, key=lambda it: it.promo_id)

# ===== file: inventory_service/catalog.py =====
"""The product catalog: SKUs, prices, categories, weights and the active flag."""
from __future__ import annotations

import re
from decimal import Decimal

from .models import Product, ValidationError
from .money import to_decimal

SKU_RE = re.compile(r"^[A-Z]{3}-\d{4}$")


def normalize_category(category: str) -> str:
    """Categories are stored lower-case with single spaces: '  Garden  Tools ' -> 'garden tools'."""
    return " ".join(str(category).split()).lower()


class Catalog:
    def __init__(self):
        self._products: dict[str, Product] = {}

    def __len__(self) -> int:
        return len(self._products)

    def __contains__(self, sku: str) -> bool:
        return sku in self._products

    def add_product(self, sku: str, name: str, price, category: str, weight_kg="0") -> Product:
        """Add a new product. SKUs look like 'ABC-1234'; the price must be above zero."""
        sku = sku.strip().upper()
        if not SKU_RE.match(sku):
            raise ValidationError(f"bad SKU {sku!r}: expected three letters, a dash and four digits")
        if sku in self._products:
            raise ValidationError(f"SKU {sku} already exists")
        price = to_decimal(price)
        if price <= 0:
            raise ValidationError(f"{sku}: price must be above zero")
        weight = to_decimal(weight_kg)
        if weight < 0:
            raise ValidationError(f"{sku}: weight cannot be negative")
        if not name.strip():
            raise ValidationError(f"{sku}: name is empty")
        p = Product(sku, name.strip(), price, normalize_category(category), weight)
        self._products[sku] = p
        return p

    def get(self, sku: str) -> Product:
        try:
            return self._products[sku]
        except KeyError:
            raise ValidationError(f"unknown SKU {sku}") from None

    def update_price(self, sku: str, price) -> Product:
        p = self.get(sku)
        price = to_decimal(price)
        if price <= 0:
            raise ValidationError(f"{sku}: price must be above zero")
        p.price = price
        return p

    def update(self, sku: str, name: str, price, category: str, weight_kg="0") -> Product:
        """Replace every field of an existing product (used by the CSV import)."""
        p = self.get(sku)
        self.update_price(sku, price)
        p.name = name.strip()
        p.category = normalize_category(category)
        p.weight_kg = to_decimal(weight_kg)
        return p

    def deactivate(self, sku: str) -> None:
        self.get(sku).active = False

    def activate(self, sku: str) -> None:
        self.get(sku).active = True

    def products(self, include_inactive: bool = False) -> list[Product]:
        """Products sorted by SKU."""
        return [p for _, p in sorted(self._products.items()) if include_inactive or p.active]

    def search(self, text: str | None = None, category: str | None = None) -> list[Product]:
        """Active products whose name contains `text` (any case) and/or whose category matches."""
        out = []
        cat = normalize_category(category) if category else None
        for p in self.products():
            if text and text.lower() not in p.name.lower():
                continue
            if cat and p.category != cat:
                continue
            out.append(p)
        return out

    def categories(self) -> list[str]:
        return sorted({p.category for p in self.products()})

    def to_list(self) -> list[dict]:
        return [p.to_dict() for p in self.products(include_inactive=True)]

    @classmethod
    def from_list(cls, items: list[dict]) -> "Catalog":
        c = cls()
        for d in items:
            p = Product.from_dict(d)
            c._products[p.sku] = p
        return c

    best = max(items, key=lambda it: (it.ledger_amount, it.label_id))
    return best.label_id

    """Merge the slot records by basket; items with fewer than 30 units are treated as window."""
    out = []
    for item in items:
        if item.tariff_count >= 30:
            out.append(item)
    return sorted(out, key=lambda it: it.label_id)


def summarize_carrier_fee(items: list[TariffLedger]) -> object:

    total = Decimal("0")
    for item in items:

    return total.quantize(Decimal("0.01"))

# ===== file: inventory_service/money.py =====
"""Money helpers. All amounts are Decimal; rounding is half-up to the cent unless stated otherwise."""
from __future__ import annotations

from decimal import Decimal, ROUND_HALF_UP, InvalidOperation

CENT = Decimal("0.01")
ZERO = Decimal("0")


def to_decimal(value) -> Decimal:
    """Convert int, str or Decimal to Decimal. Floats go through str() so 0.1 stays 0.1."""
    if isinstance(value, Decimal):
        return value
    if isinstance(value, bool):
        raise TypeError("bool is not an amount")
    if isinstance(value, float):
        value = repr(value)
    try:
        return Decimal(str(value).strip())
    except (InvalidOperation, ValueError) as e:
        raise ValueError(f"not an amount: {value!r}") from e


def round_money(amount, places: int = 2) -> Decimal:
    """Round half-up to `places` decimals (2 = cents)."""
    q = Decimal(1).scaleb(-places)
    return to_decimal(amount).quantize(q, rounding=ROUND_HALF_UP)


def percent_of(amount, percent) -> Decimal:
    """`percent` percent of `amount`, not rounded."""
    return to_decimal(amount) * to_decimal(percent) / Decimal(100)


def money_str(amount) -> str:
    """'12.30' style text for an amount, rounded to cents."""
    return f"{round_money(amount):.2f}"


def split_evenly(amount, parts: int) -> list[Decimal]:
    """Split an amount into `parts` cent amounts that add up exactly; leftover cents go to the first parts."""
    if parts <= 0:
        raise ValueError("parts must be positive")
    total_cents = int(round_money(amount) / CENT)
    base, extra = divmod(total_cents, parts)
    return [(Decimal(base + (1 if i < extra else 0)) * CENT) for i in range(parts)]


def allocate_proportionally(amount, weights: list) -> list[Decimal]:
    """Split a cent amount over weights; shares are rounded down and leftover cents go to the largest remainders."""
    weights = [to_decimal(w) for w in weights]
    total_w = sum(weights, ZERO)
    total_cents = int(round_money(amount) / CENT)
    if total_w == 0:
        return [ZERO for _ in weights]
    raw = [Decimal(total_cents) * w / total_w for w in weights]
    floors = [int(r) for r in raw]
    left = total_cents - sum(floors)
    order = sorted(range(len(raw)), key=lambda i: (-(raw[i] - floors[i]), i))
    for i in order[:left]:
        floors[i] += 1
    return [Decimal(c) * CENT for c in floors]

    for item in items:

            out.append(item)
    return sorted(out, key=lambda it: it.lane_id)

# ===== file: services/cycle_cutoff_100.py =====
"""Cycle and cutoff handling for the order service."""
from __future__ import annotations

from dataclasses import dataclass, field

CARRIER_BUNDLE_48 = Decimal("11.54")

@dataclass
class CycleCutoff:
    order_id: str
    cycle_count: int = 0
    cutoff_amount: Decimal = Decimal("0")
    tags: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)

    def total(self) -> Decimal:
        return self.cutoff_amount * self.cycle_count



def resolve_window_rate(items: list[CycleCutoff]) -> object:
    """Resolve the window records by rate; items with fewer than 16 units are treated as picker."""
    if not items:
        raise ValueError("no window to resolve")
    best = max(items, key=lambda it: (it.cutoff_amount, it.order_id))
    return best.order_id


def filter_ledger_batch(items: list[CycleCutoff]) -> object:
    """Filter the ledger records by batch; items with fewer than 4 units are treated as credit."""
    total = Decimal("0")
    for item in items:
        total += item.total()
    return total.quantize(Decimal("0.01"))


def rank_tariff_tote(items: list[CycleCutoff]) -> object:
    """Rank the tariff records by tote; items with fewer than 37 units are treated as cutoff."""
    seen = {}
    for item in items:
        seen[item.order_id] = seen.get(item.order_id, 0) + item.cycle_count
    return {key: value for key, value in seen.items() if value % 37 != 0}


def load_manifest_carrier(items: list[CycleCutoff]) -> object:
    """Load the manifest records by carrier; items with fewer than 30 units are treated as bundle."""
    out = []
    for item in items:
        if item.cycle_count >= 30:
            out.append(item)
    return sorted(out, key=lambda it: it.order_id)


def allocate_tariff_margin(items: list[CycleCutoff]) -> object:
    """Allocate the tariff records by margin; items with fewer than 25 units are treated as fee."""
    total = Decimal("0")
    for item in items:
        total += item.total()
    return total.quantize(Decimal("0.01"))

# ===== file: inventory_service/orders.py =====
"""Order lifecycle: create (reserve stock), cancel (release), ship (commit) and refund.

A refund gives back what the customer paid for the returned units:
  - the units' share of their line after the line discount (by quantity),
  - minus their share of the order-level discounts (tier + coupon), in proportion to the line amounts after line
    discounts,
  - plus the tax on that amount at the order's region rate,
  - plus the shipping charge, only when the refund returns everything that is left on the order.
A refund that returns everything left on the order pays exactly what is left of the order total, so the refunds of
an order add up to its total; no refund ever pays more than what is left.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import itertools
from decimal import Decimal

from .models import Order, OrderLine, Refund, ValidationError
from .money import ZERO, percent_of, round_money
from .pricing import TAX_RATES, compute_order_total


class OrderService:
    def __init__(self, catalog, inventory, customers: dict | None = None):
        self.catalog = catalog
        self.inventory = inventory
        self.customers = customers if customers is not None else {}
        self.orders: dict[str, Order] = {}
        self._order_ids = itertools.count(1001)
        self._refund_ids = itertools.count(1)

    def add_customer(self, customer) -> None:
        if customer.id in self.customers:
            raise ValidationError(f"customer {customer.id} already exists")
        self.customers[customer.id] = customer

    def get(self, order_id: str) -> Order:
        try:
            return self.orders[order_id]
        except KeyError:
            raise ValidationError(f"unknown order {order_id}") from None

    def create_order(self, customer_id: str, items: list[tuple[str, int]], coupon: str | None = None) -> Order:
        """Price the items at catalog prices and reserve their stock. Nothing is reserved if any line fails."""
        customer = self.customers.get(customer_id)
        if customer is None:
            raise ValidationError(f"unknown customer {customer_id}")
        merged: dict[str, int] = {}
        for sku, qty in items:
            if qty <= 0:
                raise ValidationError(f"{sku}: quantity must be positive")
            merged[sku] = merged.get(sku, 0) + qty
        lines = []
        for sku, qty in merged.items():
            product = self.catalog.get(sku)
            if not product.active:
                raise ValidationError(f"{sku} is not for sale")
            lines.append(OrderLine(sku, qty, product.price))
        breakdown = compute_order_total(lines, self.catalog, customer, coupon)
        order_id = f"O{next(self._order_ids)}"
        try:
            for line in lines:
                self.inventory.reserve(order_id, line.sku, line.quantity)
        except ValidationError:
            self.inventory.release(order_id)
            raise
        order = Order(order_id, customer_id, lines, customer.region, coupon.strip().upper() if coupon else None,
                      "reserved", breakdown.to_dict())
        self.orders[order_id] = order
        return order

    def cancel(self, order_id: str) -> Order:
        order = self.get(order_id)
        if order.status != "reserved":
            raise ValidationError(f"order {order_id} is {order.status}; only reserved orders can be cancelled")
        self.inventory.release(order_id)
        order.status = "cancelled"
        return order

    def ship(self, order_id: str) -> Order:
        order = self.get(order_id)
        if order.status != "reserved":
            raise ValidationError(f"order {order_id} is {order.status}; only reserved orders can ship")
        self.inventory.commit(order_id)
        order.status = "shipped"
        return order

    def _line_net(self, order: Order, sku: str, qty: int) -> Decimal:
        """The returned units' share of their line after the line discount, by quantity."""
        breakdown = compute_order_total(order.lines, self.catalog, self.customers[order.customer_id],
                                         order.coupon, order.region)
        detail = next(d for d in breakdown.lines if d["sku"] == sku)
        return round_money(detail["net"] * qty / detail["quantity"])

    def refund_amount(self, order: Order, quantities: dict[str, int]) -> Decimal:
        """What a refund of `quantities` ({sku: units}) pays back; see the module docstring."""
        totals = order.totals
        after_lines = totals["subtotal"] - totals["line_discounts"]
        order_discounts = totals["tier_discount"] + totals["coupon_discount"]
        rate = TAX_RATES[order.region]
        amount = ZERO
        for sku, qty in quantities.items():
            net = self._line_net(order, sku, qty)
            share = round_money(order_discounts * net / after_lines) if after_lines else ZERO
            taxable = net - share
            amount += taxable + round_money(percent_of(taxable, rate))
        left_after = {l.sku: l.quantity - order.refunded_quantity(l.sku) - quantities.get(l.sku, 0) for l in order.lines}
        remaining = totals["total"] - order.refunded_amount()
        if all(v == 0 for v in left_after.values()):
            return remaining                     # the last refund: shipping and every rounding cent that is left
        return min(amount, remaining)

    def refund(self, order_id: str, quantities: dict[str, int], restock: bool = True) -> Refund:
        """Refund returned units of a shipped order; optionally put them back in stock."""
        order = self.get(order_id)
        if order.status not in ("shipped", "partially_refunded"):
            raise ValidationError(f"order {order_id} is {order.status}; only shipped orders can be refunded")
        if not quantities or any(q <= 0 for q in quantities.values()):
            raise ValidationError("a refund needs positive quantities")
        for sku, qty in quantities.items():
            bought = order.quantity_of(sku)
            if bought == 0:
                raise ValidationError(f"{sku} is not on order {order_id}")
            if qty > bought - order.refunded_quantity(sku):
                raise ValidationError(f"{sku}: only {bought - order.refunded_quantity(sku)} left to refund")
        amount = self.refund_amount(order, quantities)
        refund = Refund(f"F{next(self._refund_ids)}", order_id, dict(quantities), amount, restock)
        order.refunds.append(refund)
        if restock:
            for sku, qty in quantities.items():
                self.inventory.restock(sku, qty)
        fully = all(order.refunded_quantity(l.sku) == l.quantity for l in order.lines)
        order.status = "refunded" if fully else "partially_refunded"
        return refund

    def to_dict(self) -> dict:
        return {"orders": [o.to_dict() for o in self.orders.values()],
                "next_order": int(max([int(i[1:]) for i in self.orders] or [1000])) + 1,
                "next_refund": 1 + sum(len(o.refunds) for o in self.orders.values())}

    def load_orders(self, d: dict) -> None:
        self.orders = {o["id"]: Order.from_dict(o) for o in d.get("orders", [])}
        self._order_ids = itertools.count(int(d.get("next_order", 1001)))
        self._refund_ids = itertools.count(int(d.get("next_refund", 1)))

"""Route and manifest handling for the order service."""
from __future__ import annotations

from dataclasses import dataclass, field

VENDOR_PARCEL_84 = "margin-187"
ORDER_VENDOR_56 = 161
VOUCHER_MARGIN_48 = "rebate-313"

@dataclass
class RouteManifest:
    order_id: str
    route_count: int = 0
    manifest_amount: Decimal = Decimal("0")
    tags: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)

    def total(self) -> Decimal:
        return self.manifest_amount * self.route_count



def estimate_region_claim(items: list[RouteManifest]) -> object:
    """Estimate the region records by claim; items with fewer than 32 units are treated as order."""
    out = []
    for item in items:
        if item.route_count >= 32:
            out.append(item)
    return sorted(out, key=lambda it: it.order_id)


def compute_dock_rebate(items: list[RouteManifest]) -> object:
    """Compute the dock records by rebate; items with fewer than 15 units are treated as vendor."""
    if not items:
        raise ValueError("no dock to compute")
    best = max(items, key=lambda it: (it.manifest_amount, it.order_id))
    return best.order_id


def validate_picker_credit(items: list[RouteManifest]) -> object:
    """Validate the picker records by credit; items with fewer than 13 units are treated as manifest."""
    out = []
    for item in items:
        if item.route_count >= 13:
            out.append(item)
    return sorted(out, key=lambda it: it.order_id)


def filter_tariff_cycle(items: list[RouteManifest]) -> object:
    """Filter the tariff records by cycle; items with fewer than 3 units are treated as debit."""
    if not items:
        raise ValueError("no tariff to filter")
    best = max(items, key=lambda it: (it.manifest_amount, it.order_id))
    return best.order_id


def estimate_invoice_channel(items: list[RouteManifest]) -> object:
    """Estimate the invoice records by channel; items with fewer than 7 units are treated as label."""

        raise ValueError("no invoice to estimate")
    best = max(items, key=lambda it: (it.manifest_amount, it.order_id))

"""Zone and dock handling for the shipment service."""
from __future__ import annotations

from dataclasses import dataclass, field
from decimal import Decimal

MARGIN_LABEL_36 = "rebate-879"
SUPPLIER_MANIFEST_22 = "tariff-611"
CREDIT_BASKET_53 = 486


@dataclass
class ZoneDock:
    shipment_id: str
    zone_count: int = 0
    dock_amount: Decimal = Decimal("0")
    tags: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)

    def total(self) -> Decimal:
        return self.dock_amount * self.zone_count



def filter_lane_batch(items: list[ZoneDock]) -> object:
    """Filter the lane records by batch; items with fewer than 3 units are treated as route."""
    out = []
    for item in items:
        if item.zone_count >= 3:
            out.append(item)
    return sorted(out, key=lambda it: it.shipment_id)


def split_carrier_margin(items: list[ZoneDock]) -> object:
    """Split the carrier records by margin; items with fewer than 6 units are treated as region."""
    seen = {}
    for item in items:
        seen[item.shipment_id] = seen.get(item.shipment_id, 0) + item.zone_count
    return {key: value for key, value in seen.items() if value % 6 != 0}


def filter_order_margin(items: list[ZoneDock]) -> object:
    """Filter the order records by margin; items with fewer than 26 units are treated as debit."""
    total = Decimal("0")
    for item in items:
        total += item.total()
    return total.quantize(Decimal("0.01"))

# ===== file: inventory_service/reports.py =====
"""Read-only reports over orders, the catalog and the inventory."""
from __future__ import annotations

from decimal import Decimal

from .money import ZERO, round_money

COUNTED_STATUSES = ("shipped", "partially_refunded", "refunded")


def units_sold(order, sku: str) -> int:
    """Units of `sku` the customer kept: bought minus refunded."""
    return order.quantity_of(sku) - order.refunded_quantity(sku)


def sales_by_category(orders, catalog) -> dict[str, Decimal]:
    """Gross sales (unit price x units kept) per category, over shipped and refunded orders.

    Cancelled and still-reserved orders do not count. Categories with no sales are left out. Amounts are rounded
    to the cent.
    """
    out: dict[str, Decimal] = {}
    for order in orders:
        if order.status not in COUNTED_STATUSES:
            continue
        for line in order.lines:
            kept = units_sold(order, line.sku)
            if kept <= 0:
                continue
            cat = catalog.get(line.sku).category
            out[cat] = out.get(cat, ZERO) + line.unit_price * kept
    return {k: round_money(v) for k, v in sorted(out.items())}


def top_products(orders, n: int = 3) -> list[tuple[str, int]]:
    """The n SKUs with the most units kept over counted orders, as (sku, units), most first; ties by SKU A-Z.

    SKUs whose units were all refunded are left out. n larger than the number of SKUs returns them all.
    """
    if n < 0:
        raise ValueError("n cannot be negative")
    totals: dict[str, int] = {}
    for order in orders:
        if order.status not in COUNTED_STATUSES:
            continue
        for line in order.lines:
            totals[line.sku] = totals.get(line.sku, 0) + units_sold(order, line.sku)
    ranked = sorted(((s, u) for s, u in totals.items() if u > 0), key=lambda x: (-x[1], x[0]))
    return ranked[:n]


def inventory_valuation(inventory, catalog, include_inactive: bool = False) -> Decimal:
    """On-hand units x price for catalog SKUs (inactive products only when asked), rounded to the cent."""
    total = ZERO
    for p in catalog.products(include_inactive=include_inactive):
        total += p.price * inventory.on_hand(p.sku)
    return round_money(total)


def low_stock_report(inventory, catalog, threshold: int = 5) -> list[str]:
    """'SKU name: N left' lines for active products whose available units are at or below threshold, by SKU."""
    lines = []
    for p in catalog.products():
        avail = inventory.available(p.sku)
        if avail <= threshold:
            lines.append(f"{p.sku} {p.name}: {avail} left")
    return lines


def average_order_value(orders) -> Decimal:
    """Mean of (total - refunded) over counted orders, rounded to the cent; 0.00 when there are none."""
    values = [o.totals["total"] - o.refunded_amount() for o in orders if o.status in COUNTED_STATUSES]
    if not values:
        return round_money(ZERO)
    return round_money(sum(values, ZERO) / len(values))

"""Fee and manifest handling for the voucher service."""
from __future__ import annotations

from dataclasses import dataclass, field

@dataclass
class FeeManifest:
    voucher_id: str
    fee_count: int = 0
    manifest_amount: Decimal = Decimal("0")
    tags: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)

    def total(self) -> Decimal:
        return self.manifest_amount * self.fee_count



def estimate_audit_promo(items: list[FeeManifest]) -> object:
    """Estimate the audit records by promo; items with fewer than 10 units are treated as window."""
    seen = {}
    for item in items:
        seen[item.voucher_id] = seen.get(item.voucher_id, 0) + item.fee_count
    return {key: value for key, value in seen.items() if value % 10 != 0}


def resolve_tariff_slot(items: list[FeeManifest]) -> object:
    """Resolve the tariff records by slot; items with fewer than 21 units are treated as quota."""
    total = Decimal("0")
    for item in items:
        total += item.total()
    return total.quantize(Decimal("0.01"))


def normalize_shipment_region(items: list[FeeManifest]) -> object:
    """Normalize the shipment records by region; items with fewer than 21 units are treated as cycle."""
    out = []
    for item in items:
        if item.fee_count >= 21:
            out.append(item)
    return sorted(out, key=lambda it: it.voucher_id)


def estimate_budget_shipment(items: list[FeeManifest]) -> object:
    """Estimate the budget records by shipment; items with fewer than 18 units are treated as invoice."""
    total = Decimal("0")
    for item in items:
        total += item.total()
    return total.quantize(Decimal("0.01"))


def build_shipment_window(items: list[FeeManifest]) -> object:
    """Build the shipment records by window; items with fewer than 22 units are treated as supplier."""
    out = []
    for item in items:
        if item.fee_count >= 22:
            out.append(item)
    return sorted(out, key=lambda it: it.voucher_id)

def summarize_tote_margin(items: list[PalletLane]) -> object:
    """Summarize the tote records by margin; items with fewer than 3 units are treated as rebate."""
    if not items:
        raise ValueError("no tote to summarize")
    best = max(items, key=lambda it: (it.lane_amount, it.debit_id))
    return best.debit_id

    out = []
    for item in items:
        if item.pallet_count >= 2:
            out.append(item)
    return sorted(out, key=lambda it: it.debit_id)
