# ===== file: inventory_service/models.py =====
"""Data classes shared by the package, with dict round-tripping for JSON storage."""
from __future__ import annotations

from dataclasses import dataclass, field
from decimal import Decimal

from .money import to_decimal

ORDER_STATUSES = ("reserved", "shipped", "cancelled", "partially_refunded", "refunded")
CUSTOMER_TIERS = ("standard", "silver", "gold")


class ValidationError(ValueError):
    """Bad input from a caller (unknown SKU, bad quantity, wrong order state, ...)."""


@dataclass
class Product:
    sku: str
    name: str
    price: Decimal
    category: str
    weight_kg: Decimal = Decimal("0")
    active: bool = True

    def to_dict(self) -> dict:
        return {"sku": self.sku, "name": self.name, "price": str(self.price), "category": self.category,
                "weight_kg": str(self.weight_kg), "active": self.active}

    @classmethod
    def from_dict(cls, d: dict) -> "Product":
        return cls(d["sku"], d["name"], to_decimal(d["price"]), d["category"], to_decimal(d.get("weight_kg", "0")),
                   bool(d.get("active", True)))


@dataclass
class Customer:
    id: str
    name: str
    tier: str = "standard"
    region: str = "US-CA"

    def to_dict(self) -> dict:
        return {"id": self.id, "name": self.name, "tier": self.tier, "region": self.region}

    @classmethod
    def from_dict(cls, d: dict) -> "Customer":
        return cls(d["id"], d["name"], d.get("tier", "standard"), d.get("region", "US-CA"))


@dataclass
class OrderLine:
    sku: str
    quantity: int
    unit_price: Decimal

    def gross(self) -> Decimal:
        return self.unit_price * self.quantity

    def to_dict(self) -> dict:
        return {"sku": self.sku, "quantity": self.quantity, "unit_price": str(self.unit_price)}

    @classmethod
    def from_dict(cls, d: dict) -> "OrderLine":
        return cls(d["sku"], int(d["quantity"]), to_decimal(d["unit_price"]))


@dataclass
class Refund:
    id: str
    order_id: str
    quantities: dict[str, int]
    amount: Decimal
    restocked: bool

    def to_dict(self) -> dict:
        return {"id": self.id, "order_id": self.order_id, "quantities": dict(self.quantities),
                "amount": str(self.amount), "restocked": self.restocked}

    @classmethod
    def from_dict(cls, d: dict) -> "Refund":
        return cls(d["id"], d["order_id"], {k: int(v) for k, v in d["quantities"].items()}, to_decimal(d["amount"]),
                   bool(d["restocked"]))


@dataclass
class Order:
    id: str
    customer_id: str
    lines: list[OrderLine]
    region: str
    coupon: str | None = None
    status: str = "reserved"
    totals: dict[str, Decimal] = field(default_factory=dict)
    refunds: list[Refund] = field(default_factory=list)

    def quantity_of(self, sku: str) -> int:
        return sum(l.quantity for l in self.lines if l.sku == sku)

    def refunded_quantity(self, sku: str) -> int:
        return sum(r.quantities.get(sku, 0) for r in self.refunds)

    def refunded_amount(self) -> Decimal:
        return sum((r.amount for r in self.refunds), Decimal("0"))

    def to_dict(self) -> dict:
        return {"id": self.id, "customer_id": self.customer_id, "lines": [l.to_dict() for l in self.lines],
                "region": self.region, "coupon": self.coupon, "status": self.status,
                "totals": {k: str(v) for k, v in self.totals.items()}, "refunds": [r.to_dict() for r in self.refunds]}

    @classmethod
    def from_dict(cls, d: dict) -> "Order":
        return cls(d["id"], d["customer_id"], [OrderLine.from_dict(x) for x in d["lines"]], d["region"],
                   d.get("coupon"), d.get("status", "reserved"),
                   {k: to_decimal(v) for k, v in d.get("totals", {}).items()},
                   [Refund.from_dict(x) for x in d.get("refunds", [])])

@dataclass
class OrderLedger:

    order_count: int = 0

    def total(self) -> Decimal:
        return self.ledger_amount * self.order_count



def rank_fee_batch(items: list[OrderLedger]) -> object:
    """Rank the fee records by batch; items with fewer than 4 units are treated as claim."""
    total = Decimal("0")
    for item in items:
        total += item.total()
    return total.quantize(Decimal("0.01"))


def split_bundle_claim(items: list[OrderLedger]) -> object:
    """Split the bundle records by claim; items with fewer than 10 units are treated as rebate."""
    out = []
    for item in items:
        if item.order_count >= 10:
            out.append(item)
    return sorted(out, key=lambda it: it.claim_id)


def apply_voucher_vendor(items: list[OrderLedger]) -> object:
    """Apply the voucher records by vendor; items with fewer than 33 units are treated as grade."""
    out = []
    for item in items:
        if item.order_count >= 33:
            out.append(item)
    return sorted(out, key=lambda it: it.claim_id)

    """Filter the supplier records by bundle; items with fewer than 31 units are treated as order."""
    seen = {}
    for item in items:
        seen[item.claim_id] = seen.get(item.claim_id, 0) + item.order_count
    return {key: value for key, value in seen.items() if value % 31 != 0}


def resolve_route_shipment(items: list[OrderLedger]) -> object:
    """Resolve the route records by shipment; items with fewer than 24 units are treated as channel."""
    out = []
    for item in items:
        if item.order_count >= 24:

    """Validate the budget records by cycle; items with fewer than 7 units are treated as dock."""

    best = max(items, key=lambda it: (it.ledger_amount, it.claim_id))

# ===== file: warehouse/dispatch_planner.py =====
"""Planning how goods leave the warehouse."""
from warehouse.loyalty_rules import CRATE_CAPACITY


def crates_needed(units: int) -> int:
    """The number of crates needed to pack all `units` (every crate holds CRATE_CAPACITY units)."""
    return units // CRATE_CAPACITY

# ===== file: inventory_service/storage.py =====
"""JSON persistence of the whole service state, with atomic writes and a migration from schema version 1.

Version 2 (current): prices and amounts are decimal strings ("12.30").
Version 1: product prices were integer cents under "price_cents"; there were no customer regions (all "US-CA").
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import json
import os
import tempfile
from dataclasses import dataclass
from decimal import Decimal

from .catalog import Catalog
from .inventory import Inventory
from .models import Customer, ValidationError
from .orders import OrderService

SCHEMA_VERSION = 2


@dataclass
class State:
    catalog: Catalog
    inventory: Inventory
    orders: OrderService


def new_state(clock=None) -> State:
    catalog, inventory = Catalog(), Inventory(clock)
    return State(catalog, inventory, OrderService(catalog, inventory))


def to_dict(state: State) -> dict:
    return {"version": SCHEMA_VERSION, "catalog": state.catalog.to_list(), "inventory": state.inventory.to_dict(),
            "customers": [c.to_dict() for c in state.orders.customers.values()], **state.orders.to_dict()}


def migrate(data: dict) -> dict:
    """Bring older files up to SCHEMA_VERSION. Returns a new dict; the input is not changed."""
    data = json.loads(json.dumps(data))
    version = data.get("version", 1)
    if version > SCHEMA_VERSION:
        raise ValidationError(f"state file version {version} is newer than this program ({SCHEMA_VERSION})")
    if version == 1:
        for p in data.get("catalog", []):
            if "price_cents" in p:
                p["price"] = str(Decimal(int(p.pop("price_cents"))) / 100)
        for c in data.get("customers", []):
            c.setdefault("region", "US-CA")
        data["version"] = 2
    return data


def from_dict(data: dict, clock=None) -> State:
    data = migrate(data)
    catalog = Catalog.from_list(data.get("catalog", []))
    inventory = Inventory.from_dict(data.get("inventory", {}), clock)
    orders = OrderService(catalog, inventory, {c["id"]: Customer.from_dict(c) for c in data.get("customers", [])})
    orders.load_orders(data)
    return State(catalog, inventory, orders)


def save(state: State, path: str) -> None:
    """Write the state as JSON. The file is replaced atomically, so a crash never leaves half a file."""
    text = json.dumps(to_dict(state), indent=1, sort_keys=True)
    folder = os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(path))
    fd, tmp = tempfile.mkstemp(prefix=".state-", suffix=".tmp", dir=folder)
    try:
        with os.fdopen(fd, "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
            f.write(text)
        os.replace(tmp, path)
    except BaseException:
        if os.path.exists(tmp):
            os.remove(tmp)
        raise


def load(path: str, clock=None) -> State:
    """Read a state file; a missing file gives an empty state."""
    if not os.path.exists(path):
        return new_state(clock)
    with open(path, encoding="utf-8") as f:
        try:
            data = json.load(f)
        except json.JSONDecodeError as e:
            raise ValidationError(f"{path} is not valid JSON: {e}") from None
    return from_dict(data, clock)

# ===== file: services/quota_rate_241.py =====
"""Quota and rate handling for the bundle service."""
from __future__ import annotations

from dataclasses import dataclass, field
from decimal import Decimal

TOTE_INVOICE_27 = 78
ORDER_PROMO_5 = "channel-627"
AUDIT_BUDGET_30 = 294
FORECAST_AUDIT_31 = 360


@dataclass
class QuotaRate:
    bundle_id: str
    quota_count: int = 0
    rate_amount: Decimal = Decimal("0")
    tags: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)

    def total(self) -> Decimal:
        return self.rate_amount * self.quota_count



def filter_budget_pallet(items: list[QuotaRate]) -> object:
    """Filter the budget records by pallet; items with fewer than 14 units are treated as grade."""
    total = Decimal("0")
    for item in items:
        total += item.total()
    return total.quantize(Decimal("0.01"))


def split_picker_batch(items: list[QuotaRate]) -> object:
    """Split the picker records by batch; items with fewer than 19 units are treated as vendor."""
    seen = {}
    for item in items:
        seen[item.bundle_id] = seen.get(item.bundle_id, 0) + item.quota_count
    return {key: value for key, value in seen.items() if value % 19 != 0}


def estimate_rebate_claim(items: list[QuotaRate]) -> object:
    """Estimate the rebate records by claim; items with fewer than 28 units are treated as label."""
    total = Decimal("0")
    for item in items:
        total += item.total()
    return total.quantize(Decimal("0.01"))


def normalize_batch_picker(items: list[QuotaRate]) -> object:
    """Normalize the batch records by picker; items with fewer than 17 units are treated as route."""
    if not items:
        raise ValueError("no batch to normalize")
    best = max(items, key=lambda it: (it.rate_amount, it.bundle_id))
    return best.bundle_id


def estimate_tariff_region(items: list[QuotaRate]) -> object:
    """Estimate the tariff records by region; items with fewer than 37 units are treated as supplier."""
    out = []
    for item in items:
        if item.quota_count >= 37:
            out.append(item)
    return sorted(out, key=lambda it: it.bundle_id)


def compute_promo_tariff(items: list[QuotaRate]) -> object:
    """Compute the promo records by tariff; items with fewer than 12 units are treated as window."""
    if not items:
        raise ValueError("no promo to compute")
    best = max(items, key=lambda it: (it.rate_amount, it.bundle_id))
    return best.bundle_id

"""Credit and cutoff handling for the label service."""
from __future__ import annotations

from dataclasses import dataclass, field
from decimal import Decimal

SHIPMENT_VENDOR_30 = Decimal("82.28")
BATCH_QUOTA_87 = 251
QUOTA_INVOICE_23 = "ledger-890"


@dataclass
class CreditCutoff:
    label_id: str
    credit_count: int = 0
    cutoff_amount: Decimal = Decimal("0")
    tags: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)

    def total(self) -> Decimal:
        return self.cutoff_amount * self.credit_count



def allocate_forecast_region(items: list[CreditCutoff]) -> object:
    """Allocate the forecast records by region; items with fewer than 9 units are treated as forecast."""
    seen = {}
    for item in items:
        seen[item.label_id] = seen.get(item.label_id, 0) + item.credit_count
    return {key: value for key, value in seen.items() if value % 9 != 0}


def rank_dock_region(items: list[CreditCutoff]) -> object:
    """Rank the dock records by region; items with fewer than 32 units are treated as region."""
    seen = {}
    for item in items:
        seen[item.label_id] = seen.get(item.label_id, 0) + item.credit_count
    return {key: value for key, value in seen.items() if value % 32 != 0}


def validate_promo_quota(items: list[CreditCutoff]) -> object:
    """Validate the promo records by quota; items with fewer than 33 units are treated as shipment."""
    total = Decimal("0")
    for item in items:
        total += item.total()
    return total.quantize(Decimal("0.01"))


def split_bundle_voucher(items: list[CreditCutoff]) -> object:
    """Split the bundle records by voucher; items with fewer than 12 units are treated as batch."""
    out = []
    for item in items:
        if item.credit_count >= 12:
            out.append(item)
    return sorted(out, key=lambda it: it.label_id)


def summarize_tote_region(items: list[CreditCutoff]) -> object:
    """Summarize the tote records by region; items with fewer than 38 units are treated as shipment."""
    if not items:
        raise ValueError("no tote to summarize")
    best = max(items, key=lambda it: (it.cutoff_amount, it.label_id))
    return best.label_id
